**AEO questions**


| Question                                                                         | Answer format                                        | Notes                                                                                    |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| What is NHS Continuing Healthcare (CHC) in relation to respite or holiday stays? | Definition sentence (40–60 words) then short context | YMYL: point to official NHS explainer; no case promises                                  |
| Can direct payments be used for holiday or respite accommodation?                | Numbered steps + caveats                             | Separate care costs vs accommodation where policies require it; cite DHSC or LA guidance |
| What paperwork do commissioners expect before approving funded respite nights?   | Numbered checklist                                   | Tie to commissioner checklist article; Confirm in WP: forms Restwell actually supplies   |
| How does a personal budget differ from CHC for paying for a short break?         | Comparison table (X vs Y rows)                       | Keep neutral; both routes vary by area                                                   |
| Who pays for what on an accessible self-catering break (care vs lodging)?        | Short paragraph + bullet split                       | Good for snippet; avoid property-specific kit claims here                                |
| How do I plan a carer respite break using self-catering in the UK?               | Numbered steps                                       | Link to carers guide and Kent resources hub                                              |


**Cannibalization and ownership**

- **Commercial Whitstable cottage intent** (`accessible holiday cottage Whitstable`, strong book-now queries): primary storytelling and conversion on `/the-property/`; `/resources/` links out as funding context only.
- **Equipment specs** (hoist tracking, wet room, ceiling track wording): primary depth on `/accessibility/` and comparator framing on `/how-to-choose-accessible-self-catering-holiday/`; do not make `/resources/` the main owner for spec-led keywords.
- **Direct payment** vs **personal budget Care Act**: related but distinct queries; use `/direct-payment-holiday-accommodation/` and `/personal-budget-short-break-care-act/` with different H1 angles and internal links so definition blocks do not duplicate.
- **CHC respite** vs **NHS CHC holiday**: consolidate explanatory blocks on `/chc-respite-holiday-accommodation-uk/` with the hub summarising and linking; avoid multiple posts with the same CHC definition lead.
- **County discovery** (`accessible self catering Kent`, `accessible coastal holiday Kent`): if both hub and `/how-to-choose-accessible-self-catering-holiday/` rank for similar phrases, differentiate by intent (funding navigation vs how to vet a cottage) per §2.4.

#### Run - 2026-05-10 - /resources/ - §2.0 evidence pass

**Research brief**

- **Theme files read:** `template-resources.php` (pillars: Funding & support; sections How to fund your stay, Grants and charities, NHS Continuing Healthcare (CHC), Complaints & appeals, Key contacts; Related guides links); `inc/page-meta-definitions.php` (`restwell_get_resources_field_definitions`, ACF groups); `inc/seo-content-seed.php` (`resources` meta defaults, related slugs for DP, CHC, carers, comparator articles); `inc/seo-content-seed-blog-cluster-a.php` (hub cross-links, not re-opened in full this pass); skim `inc/seo.php` (no `/resources/`-specific SEO logic beyond global patterns). **Copy anchors used for tiers:** focus keyphrase `holiday care funding kent`; meta description phrase `Funding hub for accessible breaks in Kent: direct payments, CHC, local authority routes, grants`; cluster primaries `direct payment for holiday`, `chc respite holiday accommodation`, `carer assessment respite rights`, `accessible self-catering holiday`.
- **SERP / tools pass (UK-oriented, session):** DataForSEO MCP was **not** listed in this Cursor workspace (see **Gap**). Used **web search** snapshots **2026-05-10** for three seed queries (not a substitute for logged-out `google.co.uk` mobile plus desktop). Recorded **dominant result types** and **related-search style themes** where visible.
  - **Query 1:** `accessible self catering Kent` / adjacent property intent: **Top types:** Google Travel property entities, major OTAs (Booking-style inventory), individual cottage sites and rural barn listings. **Theme:** bookable stays and amenity-led filters rather than council funding navigation (gap Restwell hub can own: funding routes plus Kent phone numbers already in template defaults).
  - **Query 2:** `direct payment holiday respite accommodation UK` / council routes: **Top types:** local authority `.gov.uk` respite and short breaks pages (examples in snapshot: Cornwall short breaks and direct payments, Coventry carers breaks, Blackpool respite factsheet). **Theme:** eligibility, annual short-break pots, receipts and accountability (align with DP cluster article and hub bullets; cite LA pages in drafts, not invented amounts).
  - **Query 3:** `wheelchair accessible holiday let Kent`: **Top types:** specialist accessible operators (e.g. Hawthorn Farm accessible holidays page), niche directories (Disability Destinations), mainstream cottage agencies (Original Cottages style). **Theme:** wet room or roll-in shower language, parking and ramp claims (commercial conflict is high; hub stays **informational** and links to `/the-property/` and comparator guide without duplicating book-now intent).
- **PAA-style themes (hypothesis until captured):** CHC versus social care funding; whether respite includes accommodation cost; how to complain if council refuses short break; carers assessment waiting times (mark **Hypothesis - validate with Ahrefs/Semrush Questions** or manual SERP).
- **Competitor / landscape note (≥1 head cluster):** For **accessible holiday discovery in Kent**, page-one competition mixes **OTA inventory**, **specialist accessible operators**, and **single-property sites**. Restwell’s differentiated lane for `/resources/` is **system navigation** (KCC, Kent & Medway ICB CHC email, grants list, complaints routes), not competing on amenity keywords alone.

**Cannibalization and ownership** (§2.4, §16 B2)

- **Funding hub navigation (`holiday care funding Kent`, council routes, CHC email, grants):** primary `**/resources/**`; deep YMYL answers live on `**/chc-respite-holiday-accommodation-uk/**` and `**/direct-payment-holiday-accommodation/**` with distinct H1s; hub repeats only summaries plus links.
- **Commercial Whitstable or Kent cottage booking:** primary `**/the-property/**` (and enquiry); `**/resources/**` never targets book-now keywords as primary.
- **Equipment and hoist / wet room verification:** primary `**/accessibility/**` and `**/how-to-choose-accessible-self-catering-holiday/**`; hub mentions equipment only when linking out.
- **Care Act personal budget mechanics versus direct payment holiday use:** primaries `**/personal-budget-short-break-care-act/**` vs `**/direct-payment-holiday-accommodation/**`; cross-link with different opening paragraphs to avoid duplicate definitions.

#### Run - 2026-05-10 - / (Homepage, `front-page.php`)

**Research brief**

- **Theme files read:** `front-page.php` (hero defaults, area and funding teaser, property spotlight links, FAQ section wiring, bottom CTA, partners strip, comparison table); `inc/theme-setup.php` (`restwell_get_theme_setup_defaults()` homepage seed: hero, teasers, highlights, intro, who-it-is-for band); `inc/seo.php` (front-page meta fallbacks, `restwell_get_homepage_faq_pairs()`, LocalBusiness or WebPage JSON-LD strings); `inc/seo-content-seed.php` (`home` slug: `meta_title`, `meta_description`, `focus_keyphrase`); `inc/seo-social-meta.php` (`is_front_page()` `og:url` to site root); `inc/page-meta-definitions.php` (field labels for `hero_heading` and related homepage meta keys); `SEO-INTENT-ONPAGE-PLAN.md` §2.0, §2.1, §16 B2 (intent map). **On-page phrases used to anchor tiers:** `Accessible Holidays in Whitstable, Kent` (hero H1 default); `Adapted bungalow for guests, families, and carers with whole-property booking`; `Single-storey bungalow on the Kent coast` plus `step-free routes, parking`; `Many guests use personal budgets, direct payments, NHS Continuing Healthcare, or local authority funding`; FAQ lines on `ceiling track hoist`, `roll-in wet room`, `wheelchair-accessible self-catering holiday bungalow in Whitstable, Kent`; highlights `Ceiling track hoist`, `Profiling bed`, `Full wet room` with bedroom-scoped hoist wording (`Full-room ceiling track in the accessible bedroom`). **Confirm in WP:** live hero or teaser overrides if editors changed meta away from defaults; confirm any third-party snippet (e.g. Homely Housing) still matches current offer wording.
- **SERP / tools pass (UK-oriented, this session):** **DataForSEO** MCP is not in this workspace. Used **WebSearch** (aggregated results, **not** a logged-out `google.co.uk` rank snapshot) **2026-05-10** for three queries below. Treat positions and PAA as **hypothesis** until manual UK SERP or Ahrefs / Semrush / DataForSEO runs.
  - **Query 1:** `accessible self catering Kent` (seed). **Top result types (snapshot):** Google Travel style property entities, individual cottage or barn sites, some generic self-catering listings. **Related theme:** amenity-led property discovery rather than council funding navigation (Restwell homepage can still signpost funding via internal links, not replace `/resources/` depth).
  - **Query 2:** `accessible holiday cottage Whitstable` (seed). **Top result types:** local operator or property site (e.g. cabin-style offer near Whitstable), cottage marketplace or owner directory pages, Restwell’s own `/accessibility/` and `/the-property/` URLs in the mix, legacy directory-style references. **Related theme:** wet room or step-free access claims and parking (high trust bar; spec depth stays on `/accessibility/`).
  - **Query 3:** `wheelchair accessible holiday let Kent` (seed). **Top result types:** specialist accessible operators (e.g. multi-cottage farm pages), regional cottage businesses, estate or farm stay pages with one accessible unit. **Related theme:** roll-in shower or wet room, parking, ramp access (commercial SERP; homepage should stay entry plus trust, not duplicate full spec tables).
- **PAA-style themes (not captured verbatim by tool):** cost or funding for adapted stays; hoist versus mobile hoist for holidays; how to check door widths before booking; carers and PA sleeping arrangements (validate on real `google.co.uk` PAA).
- **Competition (≥1 head query):** For **Kent accessible self-catering discovery**, page-one mixes **OTA or Travel inventory**, **specialist accessible operators**, and **single-property or micro-chain sites**. Restwell’s homepage lane is **one named Whitstable bungalow**, **whole-property booking**, **optional CQC-regulated care partner**, and **plain-English funding signposting** (teaser plus FAQs), without inventing kit beyond repo-backed copy.

- **Hoist, wet room, door widths, access statement downloads:** primary `**/accessibility/**` (and comparator guide for vetting listings); homepage keeps **short highlights and FAQ** that link out, not duplicate long spec primaries (`§2.1` hoist row).
- **Funding systems (DP, CHC, LA routes, grants lists):** primary `**/resources/**` plus dedicated guides (`/direct-payment-holiday-accommodation/`, CHC article); homepage **teaser plus one FAQ** only, so funding keywords do not become the homepage primary set.
- **Local days out and coast routes:** primary `**/whitstable-area-guide/**` (and beach walk article where relevant); homepage area teaser **summarises** and links, avoiding a second full local guide primary.

#### Step B - 2026-05-11 - [https://restwellretreats.co.uk/resources/](https://restwellretreats.co.uk/resources/)

**Scope:** `template-resources.php` (`/resources/`). **Page goal:** funding and system navigation hub (How to fund your stay, Grants and charities, NHS Continuing Healthcare, Complaints and appeals, Key Kent contacts per repo defaults), plus related guide links. **Facts:** Default sections reference KCC phone lines, Kent Connect to Support, paying for care, direct payments, Carers Trust and local hubs, Turn2us, CHC email `chc@kmicb.nhs.uk`, KCC and NHS complaints routes, and a contacts table. **Banned phrase:** not used. **Step A vs Step B brief:** **Primary** in §13.1 stays **holiday care funding Kent** (§2.6 pillar owner, `inc/seo-content-seed.php`); phrase **accessible holiday cottage hoist and wet room Whitstable Kent** sits in **Secondaries** for cluster pack alignment (this URL does not own spec-led primaries per §2.6 cannibal note). **Hoist wording:** secondaries list `tracking hoist holiday accommodation`; titles and metas use **hoist** without asserting tracking versus ceiling track (Confirm in WP and `/accessibility/`).

**Title variants (~57 to 60 characters with trailing brand)**

1. `Accessible Kent funding hub: hoist stays - Restwell Retreats` (60 characters). Puts **Accessible** and **Kent** plus **funding hub** in the opening segment, then **hoist stays** as a short bridge to the Step B equipment pack without reading like `/the-property/`.
2. `Holiday care funding Kent: hoist guides - Restwell Retreats` (59 characters). Opens on **holiday care funding Kent** for the worksheet primary scan line, adds **hoist guides** so the title still carries hoist plus routing intent.
3. `Kent funding for hoist breaks: guides - Restwell Retreats` (57 characters). Leads on **Kent funding** and **hoist** for commissioners or families who pair funding with equipment checks, ends on **guides** for hub behaviour.

**Meta description variants (~147 to 156 characters)**

1. `Kent funding hub: CHC, direct payments, carers, grants. Whitstable-area accessible self-catering: hoist, profiling bed, wet room. Browse guides, enquire.` (153 characters).
2. `Accessible self catering Kent coast: CHC, direct payments, carers, grants hub. Whitstable-area stay: hoist, profiling bed, wet room. Browse guides, enquire.` (156 characters).
3. `Kent funding routes: CHC, direct payments, carers, grants. Whitstable-area self-catering: hoist, profiling bed, wet room. Read linked guides, enquire.` (150 characters).

**Recommended pick:** Title **1** plus Meta **1**. **Rationale:** Title **1** keeps funding navigation first (Accessible, Kent, funding hub) so the result matches the hub job in `template-resources.php`, then names **hoist stays** as a compact bridge to the Step B pack without implying the hub page lists hoist measurements. Meta **1** stacks CHC, direct payments, carers, and grants in one breath, states the Whitstable-area self-catering kit line as the one-place USP (hoist, profiling bed, wet room) without inventing gear beyond theme-backed property claims, and uses **Browse guides, enquire** as a split CTA that matches the goal to route funding intents to long-tail guides. **Cannibal:** keep CHC and DP legal depth on their cluster URLs; keep hoist type and measurements on `/accessibility/` and `/the-property/`. **Avoid-ai-writing pass:** concrete systems nouns, short clauses, no stacked hype, no fake urgency, ASCII hyphens only.

**Human next:** Paste chosen title and meta into WP Search and Social. **Do not** edit `inc/seo.php` until a human confirms.

**Scope:** Competitor or news pivot, factual Revitalise context, one calm Restwell bridge (not pile-on). **Banned phrase:** not used. **Facts:** No invented kit; hoist type wording follows ceiling track versus tracking rule elsewhere in the plan unless WP confirms tracking.

**Ordered outline**

1. **H1 (one):** Revitalise holiday centre changes: UK facts, grants, and next steps (entity plus Step A themes: closed centres, grants, alternatives; Confirm in WP: hero string matches, single H1).
2. **H2:** What happened to Revitalise holiday centres? (Chronology short; Tier 1 `revitalise holiday centres closed`, `revitalise charity what now 2024` where dates match charity source only.)
3. **H2:** Where can I get grants instead of Revitalise centre breaks? (List plus links; Tier 1 `revitalise support fund grants`.)
  - **H3:** Revitalise Support Fund path (copy and dates: Confirm in WP from revitalise.org.uk or official comms only.)
  - **H3:** Adjacent UK grant signposts (light; no fabricated amounts; link `/resources/` for hub depth.)
4. **H2:** What are alternatives to Revitalise for wheelchair users in the UK? (Categorized list; Tier 1 `accessible uk holidays after revitalise`, `short break providers accessible holidays uk` as hypothesis coverage only if body supports.)
  - **H3:** National routes and categories (calm, no competitor attack copy.)
  - **H3:** One Whitstable-area self-catering option (Restwell: hoist, profiling bed, wet room; proof on `/accessibility/` and `/the-property/`; booking via `/enquire/`.)
5. **H2:** Can I still use Revitalise for anything after centres closed? (Yes or no plus scope; CTA: confirm current services on revitalise.org.uk; Tier 2 `revitalise governance trustees update` monitoring line only if seed still supports it.)
6. **H2:** How do I combine charity grants with direct payments for a holiday? (Flow paragraph plus internal links: `/resources/`, `/direct-payment-holiday-accommodation/`, `/personal-budget-short-break-care-act/`; CHC depth stays on `/chc-respite-holiday-accommodation-uk/`.)

**Avoid-ai-writing self-check:** Question-shaped H2s mirror the worksheet AEO table; H1 stays declarative; no hollow intensifiers, no Unicode em dash, short sentences in notes.

**PHP:** none (structure and plan only).

#### Step B - 2026-05-11 - [https://restwellretreats.co.uk/blog/](https://restwellretreats.co.uk/blog/)

- **T2 (59c):** Accessible Kent blog: hoist, Whitstable - Restwell Retreats
- **T3 (59c):** Tracking hoist holidays Whitstable blog - Restwell Retreats

**Cannibalization and ownership** (§2.4, §16 B2, legal trio)

- **Digital WCAG statement vs physical bungalow spec:** `/accessibility-policy/` owns **website** testing, embed limits, EHRC escalation; `**/accessibility/**` owns **door widths, equipment, room layout** (theme default already points readers there). Do not merge primaries or duplicate the same H1 focus across the pair.
- **Contract and money rules vs FAQ summaries:** `/terms-and-conditions/` owns **deposit, balance timing, cancellation tiers, insurance nudge**; `**/faq/**` should answer common guest questions but **link** to terms for authoritative numbers, not re-host a second full tier table as the primary.
- **Personal data processing vs analytics cookies:** `/privacy-policy/` owns **lawful basis, retention, rights, ICO**; `**/accessibility-policy/**` mentions contact and formats, not a second privacy policy. Cookie detail stays on privacy.
- **Commercial accommodation discovery (seeds such as accessible self catering Kent, wheelchair accessible holiday let Kent):** primary on `**/`, `/the-property/`, county or Whitstable guides**, and cluster articles; **legal trio URLs must not** steal those primaries (SERP mix is property and directory heavy, Web search aggregated 2026-05-10).

**Gap**

- **Live rank positions:** Still no logged-out `**google.co.uk**` mobile and desktop scrape in this session; Evidence rows that cite **Web search aggregated 2026-05-10** are **not** position-level SERP.
- **Volume and difficulty numbers:** No `**/seo-dataforseo**` export; all **Est. difficulty** cells remain **hypothesis** until tools run.

**Cannibalization and ownership** (§2.4, §16 B2)

- **County versus Whitstable commercial intent:** `/the-property/` owns **this bungalow, book direct, Whitstable address**; `**/**` can target broader `**accessible holidays whitstable**` but should **link down** for duplicate-risk phrases (Evidence: distinct `focus_keyphrase` rows in `inc/seo-content-seed.php`).
- **Spec depth versus selling features:** `**/accessibility/**` owns **measurement honesty, room-by-room verification, destination caveats**; `**/the-property/**` owns **confirmed bullet summary and lifestyle comparison**; avoid identical H1 targets for **hoist plus wet room** long-tail (split: spec versus stay story).
- **Funding YMYL:** `**/resources/**` plus `**/direct-payment-holiday-accommodation/**` own **CHC, DP, commissioner framing**; **these three templates** only **link**; do not chase CHC head terms on `/enquire/` (SERP is NHS and charities, Web search aggregated 2026-05-10).
- **Contact versus FAQ:** `**/enquire/**` owns **form submission, phone, email, timelines**; `**/faq/**` owns **repeatable booking and equipment FAQs** with links into enquiry for specifics.

**Gap**

- **Live rank positions:** No logged-out `**google.co.uk**` mobile and desktop scrape this session; **Web search aggregated 2026-05-10** is not position-level SERP.
- **Keyword metrics:** No `**/seo-dataforseo**` volume or KD; **Est. difficulty** stays **hypothesis** until tools run.
- **Automated web summaries** may misstate **sleeps count or superlatives** for Restwell; **never** paste those without **Confirm in WP** against published copy.

**Next validation**

Use `**/seo-dataforseo**` UK English on: `adapted bungalow whitstable`, `wheelchair accessible holiday cottage`, `contact restwell`, `accessible holiday cottage whitstable`, `hoist wet room accessible cottage kent`. Add `**serp_organic_live_advanced**` for `accessible self catering Kent` and `direct payment short break self catering` to verify **which URL Google associates** after title tests. Ahrefs or Semrush **Cannibalization** between `/the-property/` and `/accessibility/` for hoist queries. Editor manual checks: **google.co.uk**, UK IP, **mobile and desktop**, same five phrases, save **top 10 domains** next quarter. `**/avoid-ai-writing**` on any new customer-facing strings (no banned phrase, no Unicode em dash).

**Cannibalization and ownership** (§2.4, §16 B2)

- `**accessible self catering Kent` and `wheelchair accessible holiday let Kent` (commercial):** Primary discovery and spec depth stay on `**/`, `/the-property/`, `/how-to-choose-accessible-self-catering-holiday/`, and county or Whitstable articles**, not on the **blog hub** as the sole owner; `/blog/` supports with teasers and internal links (**Evidence:** SERP aggregate 2026-05-10 shows directories and property sites winning broad Kent self-catering intent).
- **Funding and NHS or CHC or direct payment YMYL:** `**/resources/**` plus the **single cluster URL per topic** own definitions; `**/blog/**` may announce or contextualise but must not repeat the same primary question stack as the hub (**Evidence:** §2.1 seed table and theme slug list in `inc/seo-content-seed.php`).
- `**/blog/` versus `/guest-guide/`:** Blog is **public index** (`is_home` canonical in `inc/seo.php`); guest guide is `**noindex, follow**` gated utility. Do not expect the guest guide to rank for broad informational queries; keep FAQs that serve researchers on **public** URLs (**Evidence:** `inc/seo.php` guest template `noindex` branch).
- **Physical equipment, hoist, wet room, tracking:** `**/accessibility/**` (and property template) own spec queries; guest guide ACF must **not** invent kit beyond what editors store (**Evidence:** plan §2.1 hoist row; Confirm in WP for any hoist mention in `gg_*` fields).

- **SERP or tool pass (UK-oriented, session):** `**/seo-dataforseo**` MCP not present in this workspace (tool descriptors list Tavily, Notion, Buffer only). Used **WebSearch aggregation 2026-05-10** for **three queries** (not logged-out `google.co.uk` rank positions).

  - **Query 2:** `NHS continuing healthcare respite holiday UK` (YMYL). **Top result types:** **NHS** (`nhs.uk` CHC guide, carers breaks and respite), **GOV.UK** (CHC public leaflet), **ICB or CHC hub** (eg NHS-funded care regional pages). **Theme:** assessment vs social care pay; respite types listed on NHS carer pages (not holiday-booking OTAs).
  - **Query 3:** `accessible self catering holiday Kent` (adjacent commercial discovery). **Top result types:** **Specialist accessible operators** (eg Hawthorn Farm accessible holidays), **regional cottage businesses**, **agency listing pages** (Original Cottages style). **Theme:** wet room, ramp, adjustable bed claims (high trust bar); hub stays **funding navigation**, not amenity-first booking.
- **PAA-style themes (hypothesis until manual SERP export):** Can CHC pay for accommodation away from home; how to appeal CHC in Kent; difference between DP and personal budget for respite (validate on real UK PAA).
- **Competitor or result snapshot (one head cluster):** For **Kent funding plus DP**, page-one style results skew `**.gov.uk` and NHS**, not holiday OTAs. Restwell `**/resources/**` lane is **curated navigation plus Kent phone numbers and ICB email already in template defaults**, linking to cluster guides rather than competing on generic cottage inventory.

- **Funding systems (DP, CHC, carers routes):** primary `**/resources/**` plus cluster guides; HIW related guides link out (**one sentence** teasers only on this template).

**Cannibalization and ownership** (§2.4, §16 B2)

- **Booking rules, deposits, cancellation tiers:** primary `**/terms-and-conditions/**`; `**/faq/**` gives short reassurance and links to terms for numbers if editors expand payment FAQs.
- **Equipment measurements and hoist coverage:** primary `**/accessibility/**`; FAQ **summarises** and points to spec page.
- **Funding pathways and CHC depth:** primary `**/resources/**` and dedicated articles; FAQ **does not** replace those primaries with duplicate long explanations.
- **Local area friction (beaches, buses, parking):** primary `**/whitstable-area-guide/**` and cluster posts; FAQ **local** category stays short with outward links.

**Cannibalization and ownership** (§2.4, §16 B2)

- `**accessible self catering Kent` and `accessible holiday cottage Whitstable` (commercial booking):** Primary owners stay `**/`, `/the-property/`, comparator page, and enquiry`**; `**/blog/`** supports with **editorial** angles and internal links, not a second booking H1 (**Evidence:** Web search aggregated 2026-05-11 shows property and directory dominance).
- **Funding, CHC, direct payment, personal budget (YMYL):** `**/resources/`** plus **one cluster URL per question** carry definitions; **blog posts** explain scenarios and **link** without cloning the same primary as the hub row (**Evidence:** theme slug map `inc/seo-content-seed.php`; CHC cluster HTML links to `/resources/`, `/direct-payment-holiday-accommodation/`, carers guide).
- **Spec queries (`hoist`, `wet room`, `tracking`, door widths):** `**/accessibility/`** and `**/how-to-choose-accessible-self-catering-holiday/**` own measurements; blog articles add **process** or **trip** angles only when the H1 differs (**Evidence:** plan §2.1 hoist row; comparator seed meta).
- **Archive views (`category`, `tag`, `date`):** Each inherits **term or date** titles; avoid targeting the **same** primary keyword as the **parent `/blog/`** page or a flagship cluster post without unique intro copy (**Evidence:** `index.php` archive branches).
